#### WEB-04 A failed background refetch of `users.me` replaces the whole app with a spinner and then reloads the page, also when auth is off

*Area 10 (Web client) · stability · **high** · size S-M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/context/CurrentUserContext.tsx:25-26`, `:33-49`, `:56-59`. `packages/web/src/App.tsx:54-58`, `:105`. `packages/web/src/components/KioskAccessGate.tsx:17-24`. …
- **What is wrong:** `networkClass = me.isError && isNetworkClassError(me.error)` is also true when a refetch fails while good data is cached. Then `isLoading` is true, and with auth on `App.tsx` renders `<PageSpinner/>` instead of `<Routes>`: the shell, the open station page and the registration dialog unmount, and the shell cleanup sets `DeviceManager`'s competition to null. `KioskAccessGate` blanks the kiosk the same way. Regardless of `authEnabled`, the effect at `:33-45` refetches after 600 ms and on a second failure calls `window.location.reload()`. …
- **Scenario:** the arena uplink drops for 30 s while `navigator.onLine` stays true. The operator presses the manual drain button, or the tab becomes visible again: `users.me` fails twice. With auth on, the station turns into a spinner for about 1.6 s and reloads; with auth off, it just reloads. The half-filled registration is lost, and after the reload the shell cannot open the competition (WEB-03). …
- **Fix (S-M):** Use `isLoading: me.isLoading || (networkClass && !me.data)`. Do not auto-reload when `me.data` exists; confirm IAP expiry with a probe that bypasses the service worker (`/api/version` is itself NetworkFirst-cached) or show a "sign in again" banner. The probe or banner is required, as long-open tabs always have cached data. For the cold start, persist the last known `authEnabled`, retry periodically in the error state and reset `recoveryStarted`. Test: extract the decision into a pure function and test that a cached success plus a failing refetch keeps children mounted and does not reload (no jsdom in the package today).
- **Status:** confirmed by code reading and a scratch vitest against React Query 5 and tRPC client 11: a cached `users.me` whose refetch throws "Failed to fetch" ends with `isError=true`, data kept and `networkClass` true. The unmount is from reading `App.tsx`. …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-04](areas/10-web-client.md#web-04-a-failed-background-refetch-of-usersme-replaces-the-whole-app-with-a-spinner-and-then-reloads-the-page-also-when-auth-is-off)

#### WEB-02 Kiosk readout screen prints and records a finish from cached data of an earlier read of the same card number

*Area 10 (Web client) · functional · **high** · size S-M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/pages/KioskPage.tsx:725-728`, `:733-755`, `:758-766`, `:788-803`, `:349-354`, `:475`. `packages/web/src/main.tsx:19-27`, `:76-87`; `packages/web/src/lib/offline/persister.ts:4`. …
- **What is wrong:** `ReadoutScreen` keys `runner.findByCard` and `cardReadout.readout` only by card number, and `race.finishReceipt` by the runner id the first query returns. Defaults are `staleTime` 5 s and `gcTime` 24 h, and the whole cache is persisted under one IndexedDB key with no competition. On mount React Query returns the cached rows at once and refetches in the background. The print effect sets `printedRef` and calls `onPrint` with the cached runner id; the admin tab fetches `finishReceipt` for that seq in the current competition and prints it. …
- **Scenario:** a two-day event, each day its own competition, on the same laptop. Runner X had seq 17 on day 1 and reads card 8001234 again at `/day2/kiosk`. The cache answers with id 17, so the receipt of whoever is seq 17 on day 2 prints, and X gets none. This repeats for every returning kiosk user. If X had no finish punch on day 1 (DNF), `recordFinish({runnerId: 17})` writes a finish time on an unrelated day-2 runner. …
- **Fix (S-M):** Guard both effects with `isFetchedAfterMount` (`dbRunner` for the finish, `receipt` for print) and print with the fresh id. State and test that the kiosk then no longer auto-prints or records from cache when offline. Exclude the three per-card keys from the persisted snapshot with `shouldDehydrateQuery` or a meta flag; `gcTime: 0` alone does not, because restored queries get the default 24 h. Test: extract the two effects into a hook and test it with a seeded QueryClient holding another runner id; the package has no jsdom or testing-library for a component test.
- **Status:** confirmed by code reading and a scratch vitest: a `findByCard` result seeded 17 h earlier, dehydrated and rehydrated, is returned first with `isFetchedAfterMount=false`, then replaced by the fresh row. `ReadoutScreen` itself was not mounted. …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-02](areas/10-web-client.md#web-02-kiosk-readout-screen-prints-and-records-a-finish-from-cached-data-of-an-earlier-read-of-the-same-card-number)

#### SYNC-12 Offline registration drops club, payment and person fields; the server silently skips some entries and can put a result on another runner

*Area 06 (Offline sync and publishing) · functional (data loss) · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-11](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-11-offline-registration-loses-club-fee-and-personal-data-and-drops-rejected-entries-silently)
- **Location:** `packages/web/src/components/RegistrationDialog.tsx:639-646` (offline payload) versus `:618-632` (online mutation); `packages/web/src/lib/offline/db.ts:50-57`; `packages/api/src/routers/events.ts:445-518` (`runner.registered` …
- **What is wrong:** The offline payload is `{tempId, name, classId, clubId, cardNo, startTime}`. The server reads `eventorClubId` and `clubName`, never `clubId`, so the runner is created with no club. `fee`, `paid`, `payMode`, `cardFee`, `birthYear`, `sex` and `phone` are not sent at all and cannot be recovered from the journal. An unknown class, or a card already registered, is a silent no-op that is still journaled and acked `synced`; the station shows success. …
- **Scenario:** the laptop loses its network for 20 minutes and 40 direct entries are registered. All 40 appear without club and with fee and paid 0, so the cash report is short and only the printed receipt records the money. One entrant was given a rental card still registered to a runner from another class; that runner's row receives the entrant's finish.
- **Fix (M):** send the same field set as `runner.create` (`clubName`, `eventorClubId: selectedClub?.extId`, `birthYear`, `sex`, `phone`, `fee`, `paid`, `payMode`, `cardFee`) and apply them in `events.ts`; update `RunnerRegisteredPayload`. For station-originated entries, throw (so the entry becomes `failed` with a red badge) when the class is missing, the card belongs to another runner, or a race entry resolves no runner; keep the silent path only for entries echoed from a peer. Extend `e2e/offline.spec.ts:201` to assert club and payment after sync.
- **Status:** confirmed from code reading. The verifier corrected the follow-on claim: the silent no-op holds only for the unknown-class case, while the card-collision case is worse (a wrong result on another runner). …
- **Full entry:** [SYNC-12](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-12-offline-registration-drops-club-payment-and-person-fields-the-server-silently-skips-some-entries-and-can-put-a-result-on-another-runner)

#### EVT-02 Re-sync reverts local card, class, name and club edits, and wipes a local card when Eventor has none

*Area 07 (Eventor and import/export) · functional (data loss in normal arena use) · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Related:** RES-02, EVT-01
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:1027-1031`: `claimCardNo(result?.cardNo || entry.cardNo, found.id, found.cardNo)`. `:152-159`: the claimer returns `null` for a raw 0 before the fallback is consulted. …
- **What is wrong:** for every runner matched by Eventor person id, Eventor wins on card, class, name, club, birth year and sex. When the Eventor entry has no card, the local card is set to NULL. When the Eventor class id is unknown locally, the class is set to NULL (`:1036`, `:1055`). There is no record of local edits; the only such guard in the sync is `length_source`, for course lengths. A club-name-only edit survives until another field differs, because `clubName` is not compared.
- **Scenario:** The desk gives a rental card to an entrant who entered without one, or moves a runner to another class. The organiser syncs to pull late entries. The card goes back to NULL, or the class goes back to the Eventor one. At readout the card matches nobody, or the runner is evaluated against the wrong course. This does not need any results in Eventor, so it is separate from RES-02.
- **Fix (M):** Minimum: treat a raw 0 as "no opinion" (`nextCardNo = raw > 0 ? claim(...) : found.cardNo`), and do not overwrite `cardNo` or `classId` once the runner has a `cardId`. Better: store the last values imported from Eventor (columns or a JSON snapshot). Apply a field only when Eventor's value changed since the last sync and the local value still equals the previously imported one. Tests: a local rental card survives a sync, and a local class move survives a sync.
- **Status:** Confirmed `eventorCardNo.test.ts` covers only the conflict case.
- **Full entry:** [EVT-02](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-02-re-sync-reverts-local-card-class-name-and-club-edits-and-wipes-a-local-card-when-eventor-has-none)

#### EVT-03 A failed `export/classes` request makes a re-sync clear `noTiming` on every class

*Area 07 (Eventor and import/export) · functional (wrong results) · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/eventor.ts:569`: every class starts as `noTiming: false`. `:577-585`: any rejection is swallowed, with no log. `:668`: `fetchNoTimingClassNames` returns an empty set for a 200 response with no `ClassList`/`Class`. …
- **What is wrong:** "Could not fetch the no-timing list" is treated the same as "no class is no-timing". It is triggered by any non-2xx, a 403, a reset, a DNS failure or an odd 200 body, and the code comment calls the endpoint "may be unavailable". The same update also overwrites local edits of name, sex and age limits on every successful sync. `sortIndex` and `classType` are overwritten only when Eventor's value is set.
- **Scenario:** On poor arena network the organiser syncs, and `export/classes` fails while `eventclasses` succeeds. "Inskolning"/"U1" lose `noTiming`. The readout and race payloads (`cardReadout.ts:263`, `race.ts:69`) and the Eventor upload placements (`routers/eventor.ts:1649`, `:1757`) now rank and time those children. The sync reports only a `classesUpdated` count. …
- **Fix (S):** Return `noTiming: undefined` when the enrichment failed or came back empty, and skip that field in `needsUpdate` and `data`. Log the failure and report "class details incomplete" in the sync stats. Test: a class with `noTiming` undefined against an existing `noTiming = true` row.
- **Status:** Confirmed
- **Full entry:** [EVT-03](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-03-a-failed-exportclasses-request-makes-a-re-sync-clear-notiming-on-every-class)

#### EVT-04 Eventor's result position is stored as the start number and overwrites drawn numbers

*Area 07 (Eventor and import/export) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/eventor.ts:972`, `:990` (`startNo: resultPosition`). The interface comment at `:136` says so. Written at `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:805`, `:847`, `:1087`, `:1130` and `:1175`.
- **What is wrong:** `<ResultPosition>`, the finishing place, goes into `runners.start_no`. On re-sync, `:1087` overwrites the local start number whenever `needsUpdate` is true. Because of RES-02, the start and finish times differ for every runner with a result, so in practice `needsUpdate` is always true.
- **Scenario:** start numbers are drawn, results are uploaded mid-event, and the organiser syncs for late entries. Every placed runner's start number becomes their place (1, 2, 3 in each class, so duplicates across classes). Start lists and bib lookups are wrong. No test covers the field.
- **Fix (S):** drop `startNo` from `EventorResult` and from the five writes, and keep `bib`. Add an integration test that a sync with results leaves `startNo` untouched. Fix it together with RES-02, in the same code.
- **Status:** Confirmed
- **Full entry:** [EVT-04](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-04-eventors-result-position-is-stored-as-the-start-number-and-overwrites-drawn-numbers)

#### API-08 `eventor.sync` writes runners and classes of a leased-out event, unjournaled, and can block the journal stream

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · stability · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MC-35](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-35-eventorsync-writes-runners-and-classes-with-no-lease-guard-and-no-journal), [EVT-08](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-08-eventor-sync-writes-runner-state-on-the-cloud-while-the-event-is-leased-to-a-venue-node)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:903` (`sync: manageProcedure`), runner writes `:1050`, `:1107`, `:1159`, `:1190`, class writes `:321`, `:338`; cloud ownership `packages/shared/src/cloud-ownership.ts:18` …
- **What is wrong:** the doc calls running the Eventor sync against a leased-out event "unsupported", but nothing refuses it: `manageProcedure` has no lease check and `eventor.` is cloud-owned, so a venue click is forwarded to the cloud, where the venue holds the lease. `routers/eventor.ts` has no journal call.
- **Scenario:** event checked out to the venue; someone presses "Sync from Eventor" at the venue. The cloud changes card numbers, classes and cancellations and creates runners without journal entries; the venue reports "N runners added" but never sees them. A created runner takes a `seq` from the cloud's counter (`allocate_event_seq()`), the same value the venue hands to its next registration …
- **Fix (S):** at the top of `sync`, `if (await getActiveLease(ctx.db, ctx.event.id)) throw new TRPCError({ code: "PRECONDITION_FAILED", message: "EVENT_CHECKED_OUT: ..." })`. Use "any active lease", not `assertRaceWritable`, which passes on the holder, where the sync would run unjournaled. Check for a background Eventor sync caller (the doc at `offline-architecture.md:448` lists Eventor sync among cloud background services) and put the check in the shared code path if one exists. Keep `pushResults` / `pushStartList` available during a lease. Surface the message in the Eventor panel. Change the doc from "unsupported" to "refused".
- **Status:** Confirmed (from code: no lease or journal reference in `routers/eventor.ts`). The seq collision and wrong-runner resolution are derived from code, not reproduced with two nodes.
- **Full entry:** [API-08](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-08-eventorsync-writes-runners-and-classes-of-a-leased-out-event-unjournaled-and-can-block-the-journal-stream)

#### MC-13 `eventor.importEvent` is not atomic and does not pre-check the slug; a failed import leaves the operator stuck

*Area 02 (Multi-competition isolation) · stability / partial write · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [API-26](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-26-eventorimportevent-is-non-transactional-and-has-no-slug-check-a-failure-half-way-leaves-a-partial-event-that-cannot-be-re-imported), [DB-20](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-20-eventor-importevent-can-leave-a-partial-event-that-cannot-be-retried), [EVT-09](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-09-import-and-sync-write-row-by-row-without-a-transaction-a-failure-leaves-a-half-imported-event)
- **Related:** API-27, MC-34
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:694-729`: the transaction covers only the event and meta rows. `routers/eventor.ts:731-846`: classes, clubs and runners are created one by one on the bare client. `schema.prisma:99`: `nameId @unique`. …
- **What is wrong:** A failure partway through (a card_no unique hit, or a dropped connection) leaves an event with only some of its runners. The retry hits P2002 on `nameId`. Soft-deleting the half-imported event does not free the slug (see MC-27), so the operator cannot recover without database access. There is no slug pre-check, so a yearly event with the same name also fails with a raw P2002.
- **Scenario:** importing "Vårserien 1" over arena Wi-Fi drops at runner 800 of 1,500. The event looks complete but is not, and re-importing fails with "Unique constraint failed".
- **Fix (M):** Check the slug before any Eventor fetch: return CONFLICT, or suffix the slug with the year or the Eventor id. Do the import in one transaction with a raised timeout using `createMany` in chunks, or make it resumable through `sync`.
- **Status:** confirmed from code.
- **Full entry:** [MC-13](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-13-eventorimportevent-is-not-atomic-and-does-not-pre-check-the-slug-a-failed-import-leaves-the-operator-stuck)

#### SYNC-05 "Clear last id", or a config save racing a poll, makes every ROC poll fail on the dedupe index, and radio intake stops for the rest of the event

*Area 06 (Offline sync and publishing) · stability · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-06](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-06-one-duplicate-punch-stops-the-online-input-roc-puller-for-good), [API-18](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-18-online-input-config-edits-write-back-a-stale-lastid-watermark-radio-punches-imported-twice), [MC-30](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-30-every-roc-config-write-is-a-whole-blob-read-modify-write-that-can-roll-back-the-lastid-watermark-and-duplicate-punches)
- **Related:** MC-10, SYNC-26
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/onlineInput.ts:190-195` (`clearLastId`, wired to a button at `packages/web/src/pages/EventPage.tsx:1043-1056`). …
- **What is wrong:** the first already-imported punch in the batch violates the unique index; the transaction rolls back, including the watermark advance and any new punches in the batch. `lastId` is unchanged, so every following tick fetches the same range and fails the same way. The only UI control resets `lastId` to 0, which is the jammed state. The config race does the same: a poll that commits between a router mutation's load and save has its watermark written back to the older value, and the next compare-and-set passes against it.
- **Scenario:** mid-event an operator presses "clear last id" on the Event page (enabled whenever `lastId > 0`), expecting at worst duplicates, as the tooltip says (`packages/web/src/i18n/locales/en/event.json:224`). From that moment no new radio punch is stored; the panel shows a raw database error on every tick. Recovery needs an SQL edit of the settings row.
- **Fix (S–M):** Insert with conflict skipping (`createMany({ skipDuplicates: true })` or `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) so the index and the re-import button work together. Make router writes patch individual keys (`jsonb_set`) or move `lastId` to its own settings key. Fix the tooltip text. Integration test: poll, `clearLastId`, poll again, then the row count is unchanged and a new punch is stored.
- **Status:** confirmed from code and reproduced on a throwaway Postgres 16: same index and compare-and-set, two old punches and one new one in one transaction after resetting `lastId` to 0 gave `duplicate key value violates unique constraint "punches_oi_dedup_idx"` …
- **Full entry:** [SYNC-05](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-05-clear-last-id-or-a-config-save-racing-a-poll-makes-every-roc-poll-fail-on-the-dedupe-index-and-radio-intake-stops-for-the-rest-of-the-event)

#### MC-10 The ROC control mapping set in the UI is never applied: the router and the puller use different settings keys

*Area 02 (Multi-competition isolation) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-07](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-07-online-input-control-mapping-saved-in-the-ui-is-never-applied), [SYNC-13](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-13-the-roc-control-mapping-set-in-the-ui-is-never-used-by-the-puller), [API-21](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-21-online-input-code-mappings-set-in-the-ui-are-never-applied-to-pulled-punches)
- **Location:** Write side: `packages/api/src/routers/onlineInput.ts:202-223` stores `cfg.mapping` in `online_input_<eventId>_config`. Read side: `packages/api/src/online-input/puller.ts:235`, then `online-input/mapping.ts:31-39` with the key …
- **What is wrong:** Nothing in the app writes the nameId key. Only `__tests__/integration/online-input.test.ts:210` calls `saveMapping` directly, which is why the test passes. The mapping table on the EventPage (`EventPage.tsx:883-936`) edits a mapping the puller never reads. `docs/system/online-input-roc.md:65-69, 116, 147` describes the dead key.
- **Scenario:** the operator maps ROC code 100 to Finish. Radio punches arrive stored as control 100, so radio finish times are never recognised.
- **Fix (S):** Make `pollOnce` use `cfg.mapping`, which is already loaded and keyed by event id. Delete `loadMapping`/`saveMapping`. Rewrite the test to go through `onlineInput.addMapping`, and correct the doc. This also removes the latent risk that a slug-keyed mapping is inherited by a future event that reuses the slug.
- **Status:** confirmed from code.
- **Full entry:** [MC-10](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-10-the-roc-control-mapping-set-in-the-ui-is-never-applied-the-router-and-the-puller-use-different-settings-keys)

#### RES-04 A start exactly at ZeroTime is stored as "no start time", and the draw defaults to ZeroTime

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-05](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-05-a-draw-whose-first-start-equals-zerotime-stores-start-time-0-the-no-start-sentinel), [SI-33](areas/05-si-readout-ble.md#si-33-zero-means-both-a-time-and-no-time)
- **Related:** RES-05
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/timeConvert.ts:15-17` and `:24-25`. `packages/api/prisma/schema.prisma:106`: `zeroTime` defaults to 324000, which is 09:00. …
- **What is wrong:** relative 0 means both "exactly ZeroTime" and "no time". No application code ever writes `events.zero_time`: `event.create` (`event.ts:264-272`) and the Eventor create (`routers/eventor.ts:695-706`) leave it out, and there is no UI to change it. Only the MeOS migration script, backup restore and `settingsRefresh` copy it. So every Oxygen-created event has ZeroTime 09:00, and the draw panel proposes 09:00 as the first start.
- **Scenario:** a new event, with the draw run on its defaults (09:00:00, stagger 0). The first runner of the leading class in every corridor gets `startTime = 0`. With a non-zero stagger, only corridor 0 is hit; a mass start at 09:00 hits the whole class. `class.firstStart` is stored as 0. Those runners show "-" on the start list and are missing from the start screen and LiveResults. At readout they have no start. …
- **Fix (M):** Short term (S): set ZeroTime at event creation to one hour before the planned first start (as MeOS does), make it editable before the draw, and reject a draw or edit whose relative start is 0. Proper (M–L): make `start_time` and `finish_time` nullable, with a migration, and remove the sentinel. Tests: `toRelative(zero, zero)`; a draw at ZeroTime gives every runner a start time.
- **Status:** confirmed (reproduced). Two scratch runs gave `toRelative(324000, 324000) = 0`, which reads back as 0. A grep confirmed that nothing writes `zeroTime`.
- **Full entry:** [RES-04](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-04-a-start-exactly-at-zerotime-is-stored-as-no-start-time-and-the-draw-defaults-to-zerotime)

#### RES-05 Comparisons with `> 0` on relative times drop runners who start before ZeroTime

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/event.ts:724`, `packages/api/src/liveresults.ts:421`, `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:1661-1662`, `packages/api/src/routers/race.ts:229-230`
- **What is wrong:** `packages/api/src/routers/event.ts:724`: the start screen filters on `startTime: { gt: 0 }`. `event.ts:438-439`, `routers/lists.ts:70-72` and `routers/runner.ts:369-371`: `hasStarted` and the in-forest counts. `packages/api/src/liveresults.ts:421`, `:445`, `:458-461` and `:471`: the runner is skipped, gets no start row, or is pushed as OK with time 0 and no radio splits. …
- **Scenario:** an event with first start 08:30 (ZeroTime is fixed at 09:00, RES-04). Early starters are missing from the call-up screen, never count as in the forest, show 0:00 on LiveResults and get no place in Eventor, while the local result list (which uses absolute times) ranks them correctly. The organiser cannot work around it, because ZeroTime is not editable.
- **Fix (S–M):** add a shared `hasTime(rel)` helper (after RES-04, simply `rel !== null`) and use it at every site; in `eventor.ts:1661`, pass `toAbsolute` values as `lists.ts` does. Add a test with a start before ZeroTime for each consumer.
- **Status:** confirmed (reproduced). `toRelative(306000, 324000) = -18000`, and `computeClassPlacements` gives place 0 to an OK runner with a negative relative start.
- **Full entry:** [RES-05](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-05-comparisons-with--0-on-relative-times-drop-runners-who-start-before-zerotime)

#### RES-06 Station clocks and "has started" use the server process time zone; the Docker images run in UTC

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional, stability · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [API-14](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-14-hasstarted-raceservertime-dashboard-and-runner-filters-use-the-containers-clock-zone-utc), [SI-23](areas/05-si-readout-ble.md#si-23-the-servers-time-of-day-uses-the-process-time-zone-which-is-utc-in-docker), [CRS-041](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-041-start-list-has-started-uses-the-servers-local-clock-which-is-utc-in-the-docker-image)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/race.ts:87-93` uses `Date#getHours`. Its docstring says "event's local timezone". `routers/lists.ts:50-52`, `event.ts:399-401`, `runner.ts:353-355` and `timeConvert.ts:33-46` (entry time). …
- **What is wrong:** The time of day is the API process's local time, so in the Docker or Cloud Run images it is UTC. The station clocks have no local tick: they show the value polled every second, 0 before the first response, and a frozen value without network. `StartScreenPage.tsx` uses the browser clock, so the two start views disagree.
- **Scenario:** the Docker stack in Sweden in summer. The start station shows 08:00 at 10:00 and "120 min to start" for a runner due now. `hasStarted` and the in-forest counts lag by two hours. On poor Wi-Fi the countdown freezes, although the station is documented as working offline. `pnpm dev` on a laptop set to local time is not affected.
- **Fix (M):** Store an IANA time zone on the event (default `Europe/Stockholm`) and compute the time of day with `Intl.DateTimeFormat`. At minimum, set `TZ` in the images and compose files and document it. In the stations, fetch the server time occasionally and tick locally from the offset.
- **Status:** confirmed from code. It was not run in a container.
- **Full entry:** [RES-06](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-06-station-clocks-and-has-started-use-the-server-process-time-zone-the-docker-images-run-in-utc)

#### RES-08 Backup-memory punches are stored as clock time in the ZeroTime-relative column

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-12](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-12-backup-memory-punches-are-stored-as-absolute-time-in-a-zerotime-relative-column)
- **Location:** Client: `packages/web/src/pages/ControlsPage.tsx:1678` sends `punchTimeSecs * 10`. `packages/api/src/routers/control.ts:1870` stores it raw, although the doc comment at `:1815-1820` says the column is relative. …
- **What is wrong:** an imported backup punch enters the readout matcher at its time plus ZeroTime. No push is needed for that. A pushed punch is present twice, as the backup row and its manual mirror (`control.ts:1780-1797`).
- **Scenario:** A runner's card lacks control 45, and the organiser imports the unit's backup to rescue the result. Control 45 now matches at +9 h, at the end of the punch list. The greedy cursor (`shared/src/readout.ts:343-378`) then reports every later control as missing, so the rescue makes the MP worse. Backup punches after 15:00 wrap to early morning and sort first. …
- **Fix (S–M):** Store `toRelative(p.punchTime, zeroTime)`, and journal the absolute value directly. Add a one-off repair script for existing `source = 'backup_memory'` rows. Filter un-pushed backup rows out of `performReadout`, the Eventor export and the LiveResults radio query. Integration test: import, push, read out, and the status is OK.
- **Status:** confirmed (proven from code). The verifier corrected the scenario: it is worse than first claimed for rescues, and harmless for complete cards.
- **Full entry:** [RES-08](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-08-backup-memory-punches-are-stored-as-clock-time-in-the-zerotime-relative-column)

#### SI-06 Station backup read: failed pages are skipped silently, replies are not matched to requests, and auto-clear can then erase the station

*Area 05 (SI readout and BLE reader) · stability / data loss · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `web/lib/webserial.ts:1108-1151` (`_walkBackupPages`: skip at :1140-1144, unchecked push at :1146), `:1214-1226`, `:689-717` and `:749-758` (`sendAndWait`/`checkCommandResponse` match on the command byte only) …
- **What is wrong:** a page that fails four attempts is dropped and the remaining pages are concatenated, so every later block shifts by 128 bytes. Multi-block slots then take the next card's block, and the walker emits bogus "SI5" records from personal-data blocks, because the SI5 branch accepts any chunk with non-zero bytes 4–6. A late reply to a timed-out attempt satisfies the next request, after which every page holds the previous page's data (one page duplicated, the last page lost). Nothing is reported to the caller, so the read returns `success: true`.
- **Scenario:** an operator recovers a readout station's backup after an outage, with "auto clear" on. One page times out four times. The upload gets shifted or garbled cards, and the station's backup flash, the last good copy, is then erased.
- **Fix (S–M):** check that the echoed address (`resp.data[2..4]`) equals the requested address and that the length is `5 + count`; on a mismatch, discard the reply and wait again (this also stops the late-reply cascade). An unrecoverable page should throw, or return `missingPages` so that ControlsPage reports failure. Never call `clearBackupMemory()` unless the read was complete. In readout mode, place each page by address in a pre-sized buffer filled with 0xEE instead of concatenating. Add an SI5 plausibility check (punch counter ≤ 36, series ≤ 4, number in the SI5 range).
- **Status:** confirmed from code.
- **Full entry:** [SI-06](areas/05-si-readout-ble.md#si-06-station-backup-read-failed-pages-are-skipped-silently-replies-are-not-matched-to-requests-and-auto-clear-can-then-erase-the-station)

#### SI-09 Punches are re-sorted by clock time before matching: false MP on a mis-set control clock, and broken night events across midnight

*Area 05 (SI readout and BLE reader) · functional · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `api/routers/cardReadout.ts:214-229` (sort at :227-229), `:252-254` (running time); `api/timeConvert.ts:24-27` (`toAbsolute` wraps modulo 24 h); `packages/shared/src/readout.ts:344-378` (ordered matcher) …
- **What is wrong:** card punches are converted to absolute time-of-day and the whole list is sorted by time, which throws away the card's own punch order. The ordered matcher then misses any control that sorts ahead of its predecessor. Across midnight, post-midnight punches wrap to small values and sort first, and `finish - start` goes negative, so running time becomes 0. Scenarios (reproduced with the real functions):; Course 31-32-33, and control 32's clock is an hour behind. In card order the result is missing 0, status OK. Sorted, it is missing 1, status MP. …
- **Fix (M):** keep card punches in card order and insert free punches by time (before the first card punch with a later time). Do the matching and running-time arithmetic in unwrapped, zero-time-relative deciseconds, and convert to absolute only for output. Fix `local-readout.ts` the same way, and add tests for both scenarios.
- **Status:** confirmed and reproduced.
- **Full entry:** [SI-09](areas/05-si-readout-ble.md#si-09-punches-are-re-sorted-by-clock-time-before-matching-false-mp-on-a-mis-set-control-clock-and-broken-night-events-across-midnight)

#### RES-09 `draw.execute` is not atomic, has no concurrency guard, and uses one transaction per runner

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · stability, performance · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-21](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-21-drawexecute-is-one-transaction-per-runner-journals-no-start-numbers-and-restarts-numbering), [MC-42](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-42-drawexecute-is-not-atomic-and-start-numbers-never-reach-peers)
- **Related:** RES-17, RES-15
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/drawRouter.ts:158-199`. The lease is checked once, in the `raceProcedure` middleware (`packages/api/src/trpc.ts:176-180`). `docker/nginx.conf:24`: `proxy_read_timeout 120s`.
- **What is wrong:** each runner gets its own `$transaction` (update plus journal row), awaited one after another, then one transaction per class. Nothing locks out a second execute. Because `execute` re-randomises (RES-15), a retry produces a different draw rather than finishing the first one.
- **Scenario:** A DB error or API restart during the loop leaves part of the event on the new draw and part on the old. With 1,800 runners and a remote DB, the request can pass the 120 s proxy timeout. The server keeps writing, but the UI shows an error. The organiser presses Apply again, and the two loops interleave different random orders, leaving duplicate slots and gaps within a class.
- **Fix (M):** Run the whole draw in one `$transaction` with a raised timeout, taking `pg_advisory_xact_lock(eventId)` first. Update runners with a single `UPDATE … FROM (VALUES …)` and write the journal with `createMany`. Integration test: an injected failure at runner N leaves zero rows changed.
- **Status:** confirmed (proven from code). The 2,000-runner timing was not measured. A proxy timeout alone does not half-apply the draw; that needs a crash or a DB error.
- **Full entry:** [RES-09](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-09-drawexecute-is-not-atomic-has-no-concurrency-guard-and-uses-one-transaction-per-runner)

#### RES-10 First-control spacing silently fails when classes sharing a first control have different intervals

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/draw/optimizer.ts:409`
- **What is wrong:** `packages/api/src/draw/optimizer.ts:409`: `maxSteps = scheduled.length + (booked?.length ?? 0) + 2`. The loop at `:410-432` places the class even when the last `firstControlShift` (`:126-149`) failed. The only warning is `drawEngine.ts:210-219`, for a class's own interval.
- **Scenario:** two classes with the same first control, a 1:00 gap and 2 corridors. Every case below ends with two runners from different classes sent to the same control in the same second, with no warning. All of the inputs can be set in `DrawPanel`. | Class A | Class B | Pairs closer than the gap | Minimum gap |; |---|---|---|---|; | 30 × 3:00 | 30 × 2:00 | 10 | 0 |; | 40 × 2:00 | 40 × 1:30 | 18 | 0 | …
- **Fix (S–M):** when a shift fails, jump straight past the last booked slot plus the gap, or loop with a bound proportional to booked × runners. A bound of 100,000 cleared every case. Have the optimizer return unresolved conflicts as warnings, and add the mixed-interval case to `optimizer.test.ts`.
- **Status:** confirmed (reproduced independently by reviewer and verifier).
- **Full entry:** [RES-10](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-10-first-control-spacing-silently-fails-when-classes-sharing-a-first-control-have-different-intervals)

#### RES-11 The result list does not share places on ties, and disagrees with the receipt and Eventor

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional, engineering · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MC-14](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-14-listsresultlist-gives-tied-runners-consecutive-places), [DB-19](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-19-result-list-gives-tied-runners-sequential-places), [CRS-011](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-011-result-list-gives-tied-runners-different-places-and-ranks-no-timing-classes)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/lists.ts:92-97`: `findMany` with no `orderBy`. `lists.ts:181-200`: `entry.place++` at `:200`; classless key at `:192`; `runningTime > 0` at `:194`. …
- **What is wrong:** Equal times get places 1 and 2, in Postgres row order, which can flip between refreshes. The Eventor upload gives 1, 1, 3, and the receipt gives both rank 1. The list ignores `Class.noTiming`, so it ranks no-timing classes that the upload leaves unplaced. The receipt ignores the no-timing-leg adjustment. Classless runners are ranked together as one class. The "unofficial" badge never shows.
- **Scenario:** two runners in H21 at 45:12. The results page and the speaker show one as 1st and one as 2nd, Eventor shows both 1st, and each runner's receipt says 1st. On the next refresh the 1st and 2nd may swap.
- **Fix (S–M):** build the result list per class with `computeClassPlacements`, passing `class.noTiming` and setting `entry.noTiming`. Add a stable `orderBy`. Make the receipt rank come from the same function. Add an integration test for `lists.resultList` covering ties, a no-timing class, DQ, a missing class and a start before ZeroTime.
- **Status:** confirmed (proven from code).
- **Full entry:** [RES-11](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-11-the-result-list-does-not-share-places-on-ties-and-disagrees-with-the-receipt-and-eventor)

#### RES-13 Time input parses "HH:MM" as minutes:seconds, and the runner editor saves half-typed values

*Area 04 (ZeroTime, results and draw) · functional · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [WEB-41](areas/10-web-client.md#web-41-editing-a-start-or-finish-time-with-an-unparseable-value-silently-clears-it-hhmm-is-read-as-mmss)
- **Related:** WEB-40
- **Location:** `packages/shared/src/types.ts:278-287` (`parseMeosTime`). `packages/web/src/components/RunnerInlineDetail.tsx:78-96` (a 600 ms debounce), `:175`, `:181` and `:382-393`. `RegistrationDialog.tsx:624` and `:647`. …
- **What is wrong:** `parseMeosTime` reads `"10:30"` as 00:10:30. Input with one part, or empty, becomes 0. The runner editor saves 600 ms after every keystroke, with no blur or Enter commit and no validation, and the saved value is journaled (`runner.ts:681-686`).
- **Scenario:** An operator correcting a finish time pauses after typing "1", and the time is cleared (0). They pause at "10:4", and 00:10:04 is saved and journaled. They type "10:30" and stop, and 00:10:30 stays stored. In the draw panel, an interval of "2" or an empty interval gives the whole class one start time, and a first start of "10:00" becomes 00:10:00. Here the preview at least shows the result before Apply.
- **Fix (S–M):** Add a strict `parseClockTime` for clock fields: `H:MM` or `H:MM:SS`, returning null when invalid. Add a separate duration parser that accepts bare minutes. Save the runner editor's times on blur or Enter, and only when valid. Require `interval >= 1` unless the method is simultaneous. Add unit tests for both parsers.
- **Status:** confirmed (reproduced). `"10:30"` → 6300, `"10:4"` → 6040, and `"2"`, `""` and `"abc"` → 0.
- **Full entry:** [RES-13](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-13-time-input-parses-hhmm-as-minutesseconds-and-the-runner-editor-saves-half-typed-values)

#### API-11 `runner.update` changes `cardNo` without `cardId`; readout and Eventor export keep using the old card

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-22](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-22-changing-a-runners-card-number-leaves-the-old-card-linked), [SI-19](areas/05-si-readout-ble.md#si-19-runnercardid-goes-stale-when-a-runners-card-number-changes-and-a-hire-cards-single-card-row-is-shared-by-successive-holders)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/runner.ts:68-80` (`buildRunnerUpdateData` sets only `cardNo`), `packages/api/src/routers/runner.ts:509-585` (`update`), replay `packages/api/src/routers/events.ts:527-557`, reader …
- **What is wrong:** only `storeReadoutImpl` (`:588-591`), `linkCardToRunner` and Test Lab write `runner.cardId`; nothing re-points or clears it when `cardNo` changes. `performReadout` uses `cardId` when set, with no check that the linked card's number matches.
- **Scenario:** runner registered with card 111; card 111 is scanned with no punches; the runner actually runs with 222, which is read at the finish as unknown; the desk edits the runner's card number to 222. `readoutByRunner` and `readout({cardNo: 222})` return start 0, finish 0, status DNF from card 111, although 222 holds a start and finish; the finish receipt and the Eventor result upload use the same stale card. …
- **Fix (S):** prefer the read-side guard: in `performReadout` (and `eventor.ts:1612`) ignore `cardId` when the linked card's `cardNo !== runner.cardNo`, falling back to the `cardNo` lookup; this also covers journal replay on a follower where the new card row may not exist yet, and every other `cardNo` writer. If done write-side instead, do it in `buildRunnerUpdateData` (shared by mutation and replay) and set `cardId` to the live card for the new number or null. Check the unlink branch of `linkCardToRunner` (`:1240-1260`), which clears `cardNo` only and journals `{cardNo: 0}`. Existing tests assert `cardId` is set after a read (`events-push.test.ts:173`, `kiosk-finish.test.ts:143`, `testlab.test.ts:111`) …
- **Status:** Confirmed (reproduced: throwaway integration test against `oxygen_test`).
- **Full entry:** [API-11](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-11-runnerupdate-changes-cardno-without-cardid-readout-and-eventor-export-keep-using-the-old-card)

#### SYNC-17 Checkout snapshot rows and refreshed settings are written verbatim, so any column the cloud has and the box lacks breaks checkout or the refresh (PR 254 adds two)

- **What is wrong:** Prisma rejects unknown fields. The cloud is deployed continuously and the box is built once (`docs/ops/venue-runbook.md:30-36`), and AGENTS.md §7 makes additive columns the normal migration. Every new column on `events` or a `SNAPSHOT_TABLES` table breaks checkout from a newer cloud to an older box. PR 254 strips its two new course columns from `course.upserted` because "an older peer's apply knows no such field", but they still travel in `tables.course` with non-null backfilled values. …
- **Scenario:** a box built before PR 254 tries to check out an event from a cloud running PR 254. Checkout fails with `PrismaClientValidationError: Unknown argument climbSource`. The failure is clean (one transaction, peer lease rolled back), but the organiser cannot run the event on the box until it is rebuilt.
- **Fix (M):** one helper that filters keys to the importer's own model scalar fields (Prisma DMMF), logging dropped names once, used in `importRows`, `importEventSnapshot`, `refreshCloudOwnedSettings`, `applyMapRows` and `applyReferenceUpsert`; it replaces the hand-kept dropped list. Add a build or schema id to `lease.exportSnapshot` and `lease.status`, and warn "box is older than the cloud" before checkout. Test: a snapshot with an unknown column on `course` and on `event` imports.
- **Status:** confirmed by code reading; the Prisma rejection was not executed (no `node_modules`), but the repo states the failure mode itself (`dropped-columns.ts:4-6`, `docs/map-symbols.md:2249-2251`). …
- **Full entry:** [SYNC-17](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-17-checkout-snapshot-rows-and-refreshed-settings-are-written-verbatim-so-any-column-the-cloud-has-and-the-box-lacks-breaks-checkout-or-the-refresh-pr-254-adds-two)

#### MC-08 Settings refresh overwrites event settings edited on the venue box every 5 minutes

*Area 02 (Multi-competition isolation) · functional / data loss; the docs disagree with the code · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/sync/settingsRefresh.ts:26-34, 49-56`: copies every column except id, nameId, zeroTime, date, removed and the timestamps. Cadence at `sync/shipper.ts:363-366`: 5 minutes while the box holds the lease. …
- **What is wrong:** `docs/system/offline-architecture.md:100-108` and the header of `settingsRefresh.ts` (lines 4-6) say these writes are forwarded to the cloud. They are not: they run locally on the box and are then reverted. Even without the revert, they never reach the cloud and are lost at checkin.
- **Scenario:** at the arena, the operator raises the rental-card fee on the box and regenerates the kiosk key. Within 5 minutes both revert. Kiosks holding the new key get 401, and receipts show the old fee.
- **Fix (M):** Either add these paths to `CLOUD_OWNED_MUTATIONS`, so they forward and fail loudly offline as the doc says, or switch the refresh to an explicit allow-list of cloud-owned columns. Decide per field. `airPlus`, `awakeHours` and the card fee may need to be editable on the venue offline, which means excluding them from the refresh and shipping them back. That decision is Marcus's. Add a test that every `event.update` writer is either forwarded or excluded from the refresh.
- **Status:** confirmed from code.
- **Full entry:** [MC-08](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-08-settings-refresh-overwrites-event-settings-edited-on-the-venue-box-every-5-minutes)

#### DB-13 Event backup dumps a table that no longer exists and hides every psql error

*Area 03 (Prisma schema and migrations) · stability · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [API-12](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-12-event-backup-reports-success-on-a-failed-or-partial-dump-the-event_log-section-always-fails), [MC-11](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-11-the-per-event-backup-silently-omits-the-journal-and-a-failed-section-still-yields-a-successful-dump), [SYNC-09](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-09-backup-swallows-psql-errors-and-still-dumps-event_log-a-table-renamed-to-journal)
- **Related:** BLD-01, DB-14
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/backup.ts:151` (`"event_log"`), `:194`, `:203-208`, `:243-257`, `:301-309`; `docs/ops/backup-restore.md:7` and `:18`.
- **What is wrong:** `event_log` was renamed to `journal` in `20260601130000_journal_rename`, so its section is always empty. psql runs without `ON_ERROR_STOP` and exits 0. The `-- BACKUP FAILED` trailer only fires on a non-zero exit, and stderr is dropped. Any future table or column rename in a core table will silently empty that section too. The 23 `\copy` statements run in separate transactions, so a dump taken during readout is not a consistent snapshot. …
- **Scenario:** the operator downloads a backup during the race. It looks complete, but the journal is missing, and any other failed section would be missing just as quietly.
- **Fix (S):** Rename the entry to `journal`. Pass `-v ON_ERROR_STOP=1` and log stderr whenever it is non-empty. Wrap the script in `BEGIN ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY; … COMMIT;`. Add a test that every `BACKUP_TABLES` name exists and has a header row. Correct the doc.
- **Status:** confirmed (reproduced). On a private PG16 cluster, the same script shape printed the error, continued, and exited 0 with an empty section. The verifier rated this medium because the doc already admits the journal gap. …
- **Full entry:** [DB-13](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-13-event-backup-dumps-a-table-that-no-longer-exists-and-hides-every-psql-error)

#### BLD-01 The per-event backup on Cloud Run cannot reach the database, yet downloads as a 200 file

*Area 11 (Build, CI and test suites) · functional (silent backup failure) · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/backup.ts:109-121` (`parseDatabaseUrl`), `:239-263` (`spawnPgDump` builds `-h/-p` from it), `:301-308` (failure trailer). …
- **What is wrong:** the app connects through `pg`, which honours the `host=` query parameter (the Cloud SQL unix socket). `backup.ts` re-parses the URL by hand and uses `u.hostname` (`localhost`) and port 5432, so `psql` is started with `-h localhost -p 5432`. The `host` search parameter is never read. Nothing listens on TCP 5432 in a Cloud Run container. The header is written before psql starts, so the browser receives a normal-looking attachment.
- **Scenario:** before a race the organiser presses Download backup on the club's Cloud Run instance. psql exits 2 with "connection refused"; the response is a 200 `.sql` file containing only the comment header and `-- BACKUP FAILED (exit 2): …`. The organiser files it as the event backup.
- **Fix (S):** In `parseDatabaseUrl`, use `u.searchParams.get("host") ?? u.hostname` for `-h` (psql accepts a socket directory there). Export the function and unit-test it with the `provision.sh` URL shape. Make a failed dump visible: hold the first write until psql produces its first output and answer 502 if it fails before that; destroy the response on a later failure so the browser shows a failed download. This overlaps API-12 and DB-13 (psql errors swallowed); fix together.
- **Status:** confirmed. Reproduced the parts that can run here: Node's URL parse of the provision shape gives hostname `localhost`, empty port and the socket only in `searchParams`; psql against a closed port exits 2 with "Connection refused". Not run on Cloud Run …
- **Full entry:** [BLD-01](areas/11-build-tests.md#bld-01-the-per-event-backup-on-cloud-run-cannot-reach-the-database-yet-downloads-as-a-200-file)

#### DB-14 The documented event restore cannot succeed for an event with readouts

*Area 03 (Prisma schema and migrations) · stability · **high** · size M–L · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [SYNC-10](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-10-the-per-event-backup-cannot-be-restored-for-race-data-as-documented)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/backup.ts:124-161` ("FK-safe restore order") and `:80`; `docs/ops/backup-restore.md:18`, `:65`, `:90-97`.
- **What is wrong:** Order: `runners` comes before `cards` (`runners.card_id`, init `:392`), `cards` before `card_readouts` (`cards.readout_id`, `:366`), and `punches` before `control_units` (`punches.unit_id`, `:530`). None of these FKs is deferrable. Missing tables: `map_templates` is not dumped, but `classes.sheet_template_id` references it (`20261103120000_auto_templates:38-40`). `card_readout_backups`, `event_permissions` and `map_colour_settings` are absent and not mentioned in the doc. `course_maps`, `graphics`, the journal and the lease tables are admitted gaps. …
- **Scenario:** the database is lost on race day and the operator follows the doc. `\copy runners` fails on `runners_card_id_fkey` for everyone who has read out, `classes` fails on the template FK, and `controls` misaligns.
- **Fix (M–L):** Commit `restore-event.sql` or `.ts`. It should load each section through a temp table, map columns by header name, rewrite `event_id`, and insert in real FK order: `card_readouts → cards → runners`, `control_units → punches`, `map_templates → classes`. Add the missing tables to the dump. Change step 1 to `pnpm db:migrate`. Add an integration round-trip test with runners, cards, readouts and punches.
- **Status:** confirmed. The verifier rated it medium because the doc admits part of it. It is kept high because the per-event download is the only recovery path for an arena laptop (see DB-36).
- **Full entry:** [DB-14](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-14-the-documented-event-restore-cannot-succeed-for-an-event-with-readouts)

#### BLD-02 `deploy.sh` replaces the whole Cloud Run environment and drops `SYNC_SHARED_SECRET` and the tuning knobs on every deploy

*Area 11 (Build, CI and test suites) · functional / stability · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `scripts/gcp/deploy.sh:174-175`: `--set-secrets=…` and `--set-env-vars="^;^NODE_OPTIONS=…"` with a fixed list; `SYNC_SHARED_SECRET` appears nowhere under `scripts/gcp/` (nor in `env.sh.example`). …
- **What is wrong:** `--set-env-vars` and `--set-secrets` remove every variable not listed. The venue runbook requires the cloud node to have `SYNC_SHARED_SECRET`, the only way to set it is a manual `gcloud run services update`, and the next `./deploy.sh` silently removes it. `PRINT_WORKERS` and `MAP_HEIGHTFIELD_MAX_CELLS` are env-only and go the same way.
- **Scenario:** the club sets the secret by hand and tests checkout. The week before the event they follow the release runbook (`./deploy.sh v0.9.x`, `docs/releases/runbook.md:58`). At the arena "Check out to this node" fails with `PRECONDITION_FAILED "Node-to-node sync is not configured on this node"`. …
- **Fix (M):** Treat the sync secret like the DEM secret: a Secret Manager secret `oxygen-sync-secret`, bound in `provision.sh` (with `secretAccessor` for the run service account), appended to `--set-secrets` when it exists, with a warning (not a failure) when it does not. An `EXTRA_ENV` passthrough in `env.sh` for `PRINT_WORKERS`, `MAP_HEIGHTFIELD_MAX_CELLS` and similar (values must not contain `;`, the `^;^` delimiter). Make `deploy.sh` diff the live revision's variables (`read_scaling` already fetches the service JSON) against what it is about to set and print every variable it will drop. This is the part that stops the next unlisted variable from vanishing. …
- **Status:** confirmed from the code and the repo's docs (gcloud's replace semantics are documented behaviour, not executed here). Not traced: what the venue shipper does while pulls fail mid-lease. …
- **Full entry:** [BLD-02](areas/11-build-tests.md#bld-02-deploysh-replaces-the-whole-cloud-run-environment-and-drops-sync_shared_secret-and-the-tuning-knobs-on-every-deploy)

#### BLD-03 The venue compose file cannot pass the IAP credentials that the documented cloud deployment requires

*Area 11 (Build, CI and test suites) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `docker-compose.venue.yml:47-59`: `api.environment` is a fixed map (no `SYNC_GOOGLE_AUDIENCE`, no `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`), no `env_file`, no volume on `api`; header comment `:10-17`. …
- **What is wrong:** values in `.env.venue` are only used for `${…}` interpolation in the compose file; they never reach the container unless the file names them. The compose file names neither the audience nor the key, and nothing mounts the key file.
- **Scenario:** an operator follows the venue runbook against the club's cloud. Every lease and shipper call gets IAP's sign-in redirect or 401, and checkout fails. Testing the day before finds it at home; not testing finds it at the arena.
- **Fix (S):** In `docker-compose.venue.yml`: `SYNC_GOOGLE_AUDIENCE: "${SYNC_GOOGLE_AUDIENCE:-}"`, `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS: "${VENUE_SA_KEY:+/run/secrets/venue-sa.json}"`, and a read-only bind mount `"${VENUE_SA_KEY:-/dev/null}:/run/secrets/venue-sa.json:ro"`. Say that `VENUE_SA_KEY` must be an absolute or `./` path (a bare name becomes a named volume). Add both as optional "cloud behind IAP" lines to the runbook's `.env.venue` block, update the compose header, replace the vague sentence in the deploy doc, cross-link the two docs. Log at startup on a venue node whether IAP auth is on, so a misconfiguration is diagnosable.
- **Status:** confirmed from the files; the IAP rejection itself is what the repo's code comments and docs state. One overstatement removed: an untracked compose override file would also work, but nothing documents it. (reviewer A9; verifier raised it from medium to high)
- **Full entry:** [BLD-03](areas/11-build-tests.md#bld-03-the-venue-compose-file-cannot-pass-the-iap-credentials-that-the-documented-cloud-deployment-requires)

#### SYNC-14 No node-role gate: a venue box and the cloud both push LiveResults for a checked-out event, and Sheets gets every readout twice

*Area 06 (Offline sync and publishing) · stability, docs mismatch · **high** · size S–M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [EVT-06](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-06-a-checked-out-venue-node-and-the-cloud-both-push-the-same-event-to-the-same-liveresults-competition)
- **Related:** SYNC-15
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/background/supervisor.ts:49-56` (`reconcile` calls `reconcileEnabledPushers` unconditionally), `packages/api/src/index.ts:165-167` (always started), `packages/api/src/leader.ts:54-82` (lease row in the node's own …
- **What is wrong:** `docs/system/offline-architecture.md:37-40,447-449` says the pushers are "config-flagged so a venue copy never double-runs them". No such flag exists; `NODE_ROLE` is read only by the forwarder and the lease status. After checkout of an event with LiveResults enabled, a venue box with uplink and the cloud both run `syncAll` against the same `tavid` from two databases. The cloud replica lags, so its cycle deletes the remote rows of runners it still sees as Unknown and the venue rewrites them next cycle. …
- **Scenario:** an event with LiveResults is checked out to the box, which has 4G. Runners who just read out at the arena appear on and vanish from the public results every cycle until the cloud ingests their readouts; the backup sheet has every readout twice.
- **Fix (S–M):** decide the owner in code. Push only where `isRaceWritable(db, eventId)` is true (the leaseholder), or skip pushers and pullers on `nodeRole() === "venue"`, and record the decision in the doc. The same gate is missing for `reconcileEnabledPullers` and `maybeStartStorageGc` (`supervisor.ts:51,55`). For Sheets, skip `pushToGoogleSheet` in ingest when the entry was shipped from a peer node. Test in `background-supervisor.test.ts`.
- **Status:** confirmed from code reading that nothing gates it. The general "overwrite and delete each other's rows" is narrowed to the lag effect, which needs no SYNC-06; the part that depends on differing statuses was not judged. Needs the box to have internet …
- **Full entry:** [SYNC-14](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-14-no-node-role-gate-a-venue-box-and-the-cloud-both-push-liveresults-for-a-checked-out-event-and-sheets-gets-every-readout-twice)

#### SYNC-15 LiveResults push deletes and re-inserts every unfinished runner each cycle, one statement at a time, and cycles overlap

*Area 06 (Offline sync and publishing) · functional, performance, stability · **high** · size M–L · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [DB-09](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-09-liveresults-push-deletes-in-forest-runners-every-cycle-runs-n1-over-the-wan-and-lets-cycles-overlap), [RES-14](areas/04-zerotime-results.md#res-14-each-liveresults-sync-cycle-deletes-in-forest-runners-runs-fully-sequentially-and-can-overlap-itself), [MC-31](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-31-the-liveresults-pusher-has-no-overlap-guard-and-makes-one-round-trip-per-row-cycles-overlap-at-2000-runners)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/liveresults.ts:402-404` (every non-removed Unknown runner), `:407-414` (DELETE from `results` and `runners`), `:417-501` (per-runner autocommit upserts …
- **What is wrong:** Every cycle removes the remote `runners` row and all `results` rows (start row, radio splits, and a finish row whose status is still Unknown) of every runner whose status is Unknown, then re-inserts them one by one with no transaction. Between delete and re-insert those runners are absent remotely. Cost is 2 to 3 MySQL round trips per runner over the WAN, sequential: 2,000 runners at 30 ms RTT take 2 to 3 minutes against a 30 s default interval. No overlap guard: overlapping runs interleave, so run B's DELETE removes rows run A just re-inserted. …
- **Scenario:** a 2,000-runner event in the morning, everyone Unknown. Each 30 s tick deletes 2,000 runners and their start rows remotely and takes minutes to put them back, while 4 to 6 further runs are doing the same.
- **Fix (M–L):** add a `busy` flag like the puller's (`online-input/puller.ts:318-331`, S); compute the desired row set and delete only `dbid NOT IN (desired)` for this `tavid`, including removed runners; fetch punches once per event grouped by card; use chunked multi-row `INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` inside a MySQL transaction; keep a per-event hash of pushed rows and send only changes; cache the connection string, put `AbortSignal.timeout` on its fetch and set a query timeout. Extend `liveresults-pusher.test.ts` with an overlapping-tick test.
- **Status:** confirmed from code reading. Cost corrected from "3+ minutes" to 2 to 3 minutes, and the per-runner Postgres query applies only on courses with radio controls. …
- **Full entry:** [SYNC-15](areas/06-offline-sync.md#sync-15-liveresults-push-deletes-and-re-inserts-every-unfinished-runner-each-cycle-one-statement-at-a-time-and-cycles-overlap)

#### EVT-05 "Replace all" course import is not transactional; a failure mid-import leaves no courses, controls or class links

*Area 07 (Eventor and import/export) · stability (partial write) · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [API-05](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-05-courseimportcourses-with-replaceall-wipes-all-courses-controls-and-class-links-before-a-non-transactional-rewrite), [MC-38](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-38-importcourses-with-replace-all-wipes-first-and-writes-the-rest-outside-any-transaction), [DB-16](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-16-importcourses-with-replace-all-wipes-the-event-outside-a-transaction), [CRS-022](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-022-importcourses-with-replaceall-wipes-courses-controls-and-class-assignments-before-a-long-non-transactional-write-sequence)
- **Related:** CRS-005, CRS-001
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/course.ts:1883-1899`: the wipe is three auto-committed `updateMany` calls (courses removed, controls removed, `class.courseId = null`). Then sequential awaited writes on `ctx.db`, ending at `:2373`. …
- **What is wrong:** a throw after the wipe leaves the wipe committed and the rebuild partial: a dropped DB connection, a statement timeout, a process restart, or an exception in `persistStructure`, `syncVariants` or `writeImportedObjects`. The comment's claim that "a crash in between loses only the ship" does not hold for replace-all.
- **Scenario:** On race morning the organiser re-imports a corrected course file, and the connection drops after the wipe. Every class has `courseId = null` and every control is removed. Readout resolves `runner.courseId ?? runner.class?.courseId` (`cardReadout.ts:196`, `race.ts:39`), so every runner who relies on the class's course has none. No journal entry was written. …
- **Fix (M):** Wrap the wipe, the rebuild and the journal append in one `ctx.db.$transaction(async (tx) => …)` with a raised timeout, and pass `tx` to the helpers. Minimum (S): put the three wipes and the control/course upserts in one transaction. Add an integration test that injects a failure after the wipe and asserts the old courses, controls and class links are intact.
- **Status:** Confirmed Validation that can throw on bad input runs before the wipe, so the trigger is an infrastructure failure.
- **Full entry:** [EVT-05](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-05-replace-all-course-import-is-not-transactional-a-failure-mid-import-leaves-no-courses-controls-or-class-links)

#### CRS-001 course.importCourses resets status, extra punch codes and name of every matched control

*Area 09 (Courses, printing and elevation) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/course.ts:2067-2071 (written at 2086-2107; values derived at 2004-2026)`; also `packages/api/src/routers/course.ts:2067`
- **What is wrong:** For a file control that matches an existing row, baseData unconditionally writes `status` (valueToControlStatus(0) = ok for every regular control), `codes: pc.id` and `name: ""`. A control the organiser has marked Bad / Optional / NoTiming / BadNoTiming, or given a second accepted code ("31;131", a replaced unit) or a name, loses that on any re-import, although those fields are race configuration the file does not carry. resolveCourseExpectedPositions reads control.status and codes at readout and list time, so results change.
- **Scenario:** During an event control 45 is stolen; the organiser sets it to Bad so it is skipped in matching. Later someone re-imports the course file (to add a missing course, fix a position, or map classes). Control 45 is matched by code, its status is written back to ok, and every runner without a 45 punch is evaluated as missing punch in subsequent readouts and in the lists that recompute against the course. …
- **Fix (S):** On the matched (update) branch do not write status for a row that is not changing role (only set start/finish when the file's type differs from the row's kind), keep existing extra codes (write codes only when the first code differs, preserving the tail), and keep a non-empty name. Add an integration test: mark a control bad + multi-code, re-import, assert status/codes unchanged. Verifier: Matched ordinary controls: leave `status`, `codes` and `name` out of the update. Keep the stored status unless the row is start/finish and the kind is changing, keep the stored codes (the file id is already among them, since that is how it matched), and keep a non-empty stored name. …
- **Status:** confirmed. Code read only (no reproduction run; node_modules absent and the path is DB-bound). Read packages/api/src/routers/course.ts:1938-1962 (existing-control lookup and keying), :2004-2026 (status/name/codes derived from the file only), :2061-2084 …
- **Full entry:** [CRS-001](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-001-courseimportcourses-resets-status-extra-punch-codes-and-name-of-every-matched-control)

#### API-06 Test Lab mutations run against any live event: simulation overwrites real cards and results, `clearRunners` hard-deletes, nothing is journaled

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · stability (data loss) · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MC-15](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-15-test-lab-can-destroy-a-live-events-runners-and-results-with-no-lease-check-and-no-journal), [DB-10](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-10-test-lab-can-overwrite-or-hard-delete-a-real-events-race-data)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/testLab.ts:141-179` (`processSimulatedReadout`), `:243-248` (`clearRunners`), `:292-551` (`startSimulation`), `:622-729` (`generateReadout`), `:859-879` (`pushBackupPunch`) …
- **What is wrong:** no procedure checks that the event is a test event (no such marker exists on `Event`). `startSimulation` takes every runner with a start time and a card and overwrites `cards.punches_raw` (the row real readout writes) and `runners.finish_time` / `status`. `clearRunners` is `runner.deleteMany({ where: { eventId } })`, a hard delete that includes removed rows. All use `manageProcedure`; no journal call exists in the file. Not being lease-managed is documented (`docs/system/background-jobs-lease.md:161-162`); not being restricted to test events is not.
- **Scenario:** the day before or during a race an organiser opens Test Lab (in the More menu of every event) and presses Start simulation. Once simulated time reaches the first finish (1 to 3 minutes at speed 10; immediately at speed 0) real runners' card punches, finish times and statuses are overwritten in finish order, including manual DSQ or finish corrections. The server timer keeps running after the tab closes. …
- **Fix (M):** add an explicit `isTestEvent` flag on `events`, set by `generateEvent` (or a switch), and refuse every mutating Test Lab procedure (simulation, readout generation, `pushBackupPunch`, `generateClasses/Courses`, `registerFictionalRunners`, `registerRunners`, `clearRunners`) with PRECONDITION_FAILED otherwise. The heuristic "no readouts and no Eventor id" would still allow wiping a manually created real event before race day. Keep `stopSimulation` unguarded so a mistaken run can be stopped. With the flag, Test Lab can stay non-journaled and lease-exempt; update `background-jobs-lease.md:161`. Hide the tab for non-test events. …
- **Status:** Confirmed (from code; no guard, no journal, unconditional mount).
- **Full entry:** [API-06](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-06-test-lab-mutations-run-against-any-live-event-simulation-overwrites-real-cards-and-results-clearrunners-hard-deletes-nothing-is-journaled)

#### API-13 Kiosk key can set or clear any runner's finish time and status through `race.recordFinish`

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · security · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MC-25](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-25-the-kiosk-key-authorises-racerecordfinish-contradicting-the-auth-doc-and-the-journal-entry-has-no-actor)
- **Related:** RES-24
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/race.ts:320-361`, `packages/api/src/trpc.ts:260-290` (`kioskOrCapProcedure`) and `:341-347` (`kioskOrRaceOperateRaceProcedure`); doc `docs/system/authentication.md:213` ("Mutations never accept the key.") …
- **What is wrong:** `recordFinish` is the only mutation on a `kioskOr*` builder. With a matching `x-kiosk-key` it runs with `{race.operate}` and no user. Input is unbounded ints: any runner, any finish time, any status (unknown numbers become `unknown`); with no time, `finishTime: 0` is written. Runner ids are obtainable with the same key (`runner.findByCard`, `race.lookupByCard`, `race.recentActivity`, `race.finishReceipt`).
- **Scenario:** with only the kiosk key from a public kiosk URL, `recordFinish({id, finishTime: 360000, status: 1})` gives a runner with no card or readout an OK finish; `finishTime: 999999999` is accepted; `recordFinish({id})` clears the finish and leaves the status. Writes are journaled as `finish.adjusted` with no actor.
- **Fix (M):** expose from `kioskOrCapProcedure` whether access came from the key. For key-only callers refuse `status`, require a positive `finishTime` within the event day, refuse to overwrite an existing finish, and bind the call to the card (have the kiosk send `cardNo` and require it to equal the runner's `cardNo`; "recently read out" has no server-side basis because the kiosk's `cardReadout.readout` is a query). This matches real kiosk use (`KioskPage.tsx` sends only `runnerId` and `finishTime`, and only when the stored finish is 0). For every caller validate `finishTime` range and `status` against the enum. …
- **Status:** Confirmed (reproduced: throwaway integration test, caller `user: null, authEnabled: true`).
- **Full entry:** [API-13](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-13-kiosk-key-can-set-or-clear-any-runners-finish-time-and-status-through-racerecordfinish)

#### API-15 PR #254: background climb and auto-route jobs write courses and journal `course.upserted` on a node that does not hold the lease

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · stability · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [CRS-014](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-014-automatic-leg-route-refresh-writes-courses-and-journals-courseupserted-on-a-node-that-does-not-hold-the-events-lease), [MC-36](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-36-background-route-and-climb-refreshes-and-other-non-race-paths-journal-course-and-control-rows-outside-the-lease-guard), [API-37n](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-37-low-severity-cleanup)
- **Location:** PR #254: `packages/api/src/trpc.ts:170-185` (new `eventProcedure` middleware), `packages/api/src/leg-routes-auto.ts:168-231` (`refreshAutoRoutes`), `:239-256` (`enqueueAutoRoutes`), `packages/api/src/course-climb.ts:97-147` …
- **What is wrong:** the PR enqueues `refreshAutoRoutes` after every `courseObject.*` mutation (plain `coursesEditProcedure`) and after marked course changes, before and independent of the lease guard. The job writes `leg_routes`, calls `clearComputedClimbs` (zeroes `courses.climb_m`, unjournaled) and `computeMissingClimbs`, which appends `course.upserted` via `emitCourseUpserted`. Because `courseObject.` is cloud-owned, during a checkout every out-of-bounds edit, including one made at the venue, runs on the cloud, the non-holder. …
- **Scenario:** (1) the venue (holder) has edited course X's controls and the entry has not shipped (poor arena network). A course-object edit lands on the cloud; its refresh changes an automatic route of a pair X runs; the cloud clears and recomputes X's climb and journals `course.upserted` with its old copy of X. The venue pulls it and course X and its `course_controls` revert …
- **Fix (S):** in `refreshAutoRoutes` and in `computeMissingClimbs` (which also covers `refreshMarkedClimbs` with "all" from map changes) return early unless `await isRaceWritable(db, eventId)`, mirroring `dealVariantFollowUp` (`events.ts:331`). Re-check before each chunk's writes, since a refresh can run for minutes and a checkout may start mid-run. Because the holder never runs `courseObject.*` locally, have the venue enqueue its own refresh after a forwarded course-object write (in `refreshAfterForward`, after `refreshCloudOwnedMaps` has mirrored the objects), or routes and climbs stay stale until the next marked course edit. …
- **Status:** Confirmed (from code on the PR checkout at `871c74de`; not run).
- **Full entry:** [API-15](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-15-pr-254-background-climb-and-auto-route-jobs-write-courses-and-journal-courseupserted-on-a-node-that-does-not-hold-the-lease)

- **What is wrong:** `pendingOwnerDataRef` holds the SI card's owner data for the deferred name fill. It is written at `:295` (pending-card path, correctly `?? null`) and at `:398` (sticky path, only inside `if (latest.ownerData)`). `clearForm`, `startManualCard` and the SI card field never reset it, and the dialog stays mounted, so the ref survives close and reopen. The deferred effect fires for any next card once `findByCard` returns no match and the name is empty. Owner data is null for SI5/6/8/9, pCard and blank SI10 cards, since only the SI10/SIAC/SI11 path parses it. …
- **Scenario:** at a sticky desk, a personalised SIAC for "Anna Svensson" is registered. The next runner has a hired SI8, or the operator types the number. - **What is wrong:** `club.list` returns every club without an Eventor id as `id: 0, extId: 0`, sorted by name. The dialog's default `clubId` is 0 and `selectedClub = clubs.data?.find((c) => c.id === clubId)` has no `clubId > 0` guard, so it resolves to the alphabetically first free-text club. Its name is sent as `clubName` in `runner.create` (with `eventorClubId: 0`), stored verbatim, and shown on the kiosk messages and the receipt. The picker shows "No club" because that option, also value 0, comes first. …
- **Scenario:** one runner was imported with the typed club "Friskis" and no Eventor id. From then on every online registration left on "No club" is stored as "Friskis", which reaches results and club invoicing, while the operator sees "No club".

The picker is a `SearchableSelect` over that list plus "No club", with no free-text input, and `club.searchDirectory` has no web caller. #### WEB-07 Runner inline editor: one debounce timer for all fields drops the earlier edit behind a false "Saved", and a late refetch overwrites what is being typed

*Area 10 (Web client) · functional (silent data loss) · **high** · size S-M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/components/RunnerInlineDetail.tsx:73-96` (single `saveTimeoutRef`, cleared at `:80`, payload `{ [field]: value }` at `:84`, `onError` at `:90`). …
- **What is wrong:** Dropped edit. The panel has one timer. Each `debouncedSave` call clears it, and the surviving call sends only its own field. An edit to field A followed within 600 ms by an edit to field B never sends A. The indicator shows Saving then Saved, because the callbacks belong to B's call. Field A's input keeps showing the unsaved text next to "Saved" until the row is collapsed and reopened, so the operator has no cue at all that the edit was lost. Refetch overwrites typing. …
- **Scenario:** the operator corrects a runner's name, tabs to the card field and types the new card number within 600 ms. Only the card number is saved. The name input still shows the corrected name and the panel says "Saved", but the database has the old name.
- **Fix (S-M):** Collect pending changes in one map with one timer (`pending = { ...pending, [field]: value }`, send `data: pending`), or use one timer per field. In `EditField`, do not sync from props while the input is focused or dirty; clear the dirty flag when the stored value equals the local one. Better still, commit on blur or Enter as RES-13 proposes for time fields; fix the two together. Surface save errors (see WEB-08). Tests: a component or E2E test that edits two fields within 600 ms and asserts both are stored after reload; one that types into a time field with a pause and asserts the input is not rewritten mid-typing.
- **Status:** confirmed in part, from code reading of the component and `runner.update`; no test was run, as the component has no unit test. Refuted and dropped: the claim that the name field reverts to the old value after the refetch. …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-07](areas/10-web-client.md#web-07-runner-inline-editor-one-debounce-timer-for-all-fields-drops-the-earlier-edit-behind-a-false-saved-and-a-late-refetch-overwrites-what-is-being-typed)

#### WEB-08 Runner inline editor: failed saves are silent, so a rejected card number or empty name stays in the input with no message

*Area 10 (Web client) · stability · **high** · size S-M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/components/RunnerInlineDetail.tsx:74-96` (`onError: () => setSaving(false)` at `:90`) and `:129` (stale "Saved"). …
- **What is wrong:** the only error handling on the main save is clearing the spinner. `updateMutation.error` is never rendered, and the `QueryClient` has no `MutationCache` handler or toast. `EditField` keeps its `localValue`, and on error nothing invalidates or resets it, so the rejected value stays in the input. The only cue is that green "Saved" does not appear, and line 129 can still show a stale "Saved" from an earlier success for up to 3 s. …
- **Scenario:** a runner swaps to a hired card and the operator types a number still assigned to another runner. The input shows the new number, no message appears, and the database keeps the old card while the hired card stays on the other runner. At the finish the readout is attributed to that other runner.
- **Fix (S-M):** Render the tRPC error message next to the save indicator, red and kept until the next successful save. If reverting the input, force it with a key or epoch, since a refetch returning the same value does not re-trigger the effect. Add `onError` to all seven other call sites. Validate before sending: non-empty name, class greater than 0, no NaN times; commit `EditField`s on blur or Enter (also fixes WEB-07). Playwright test: set a taken card number inline and expect the CONFLICT text; an empty-name case.
- **Status:** confirmed in part, from code reading of the component, `SearchableSelect`, `main.tsx` and `runner.ts`; no test run. Refuted and dropped: a rejected '-' class does not stay on screen, because `SelectField` derives its label from the stored value and snaps back …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-08](areas/10-web-client.md#web-08-runner-inline-editor-failed-saves-are-silent-so-a-rejected-card-number-or-empty-name-stays-in-the-input-with-no-message)

#### WEB-09 Class course picker is a multi-select but only the first ticked course is saved; course changes apply on one click with no confirmation and can clear raced runners' variants
