A class has one course on the server, and `class.detail` and `class.list` return at most one id, so the list badge is dead code. Because the dropdown appends, ticking a second course re-sends the saved one, while the panel shows "2 courses selected", "Courses (forked)" and "Forked, runners will be assigned different courses". `editCourseIds` resets only when the row is collapsed. …
- **Scenario:** on race morning H21 (120 runners) is on "Bana 1". The operator ticks "Bana 2" without unticking "Bana 1". The UI says forked, the server still has Bana 1, and every H21 readout is evaluated against Bana 1. Server guard: clearing a pool must not null raced runners' variants, since `class.bulkUpdate` (`classRouter.ts:390`) takes the same path. Rewrite `e2e/classes.spec.ts:177-203` to assert the stored course after reload …

#### WEB-10 Bulk "Set club" changes only the Eventor id; the club name shown in lists stays the old one, and a free-text club strips the id

*Area 10 (Web client) · functional · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/pages/RunnerManagement.tsx:194-208` (`handleApplyBulk`), `:457-468` (club select), `:320-321` (row renders `ClubLogo` by id and `clubName` as text). …
- **What is wrong:** the bulk bar sends `{ ids, eventorClubId: Number(bulkValue) }`. `bulkUpdate` sets `clubName` only when a `clubName` input is given, which the client never sends, and does no directory lookup. `runner.list` returns `clubName` as stored, so after the action the logo follows the new id while the name stays. The same name-less patch is journaled, so peers replay the inconsistency. The single-runner `clubId` path does look up the name, so the two paths disagree. …
- **Scenario:** select 12 runners entered under the wrong club, choose Set club, "OK Linné", confirm. The confirm names OK Linné, the mutation succeeds, and all 12 rows still show the old club name beside OK Linné's logo.
- **Fix (S):** Client: give options a unique value (`e:<id>` or `n:<name>`) and unique keys, and send `clubName: club.name` with `eventorClubId: club.eventorId ?? null`. Server: when `eventorClubId` is set without `clubName`, resolve the name from `clubDirectory` as `buildRunnerUpdateData` does (best effort); normalise 0 to null; put the resolved name into `journalFields`. Tests: an API integration test for `bulkUpdate` with a club, and a club case in `e2e/runners-bulk.spec.ts` asserting the name column.
- **Status:** confirmed, from code reading of the page, `runner.ts` and `event.ts`; no test run, as the path has no branching guard. The verifier found the free-text part slightly worse (shared value and key, wrong confirm label). …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-10](areas/10-web-client.md#web-10-bulk-set-club-changes-only-the-eventor-id-the-club-name-shown-in-lists-stays-the-old-one-and-a-free-text-club-strips-the-id)

#### CRS-002 Setter maps are a frozen control list: controls added or placed after generation appear on no setter map, with no warning

*Area 09 (Courses, printing and elevation) · functional · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/shared/src/course-maps/sheet-rows.ts:293`; also packages/api/src/course-maps/auto-sheets.ts:531 (setterMaps), packages/api/src/course-maps/routes.ts:270 (setter export)
- **What is wrong:** A setter sheet draws `ctx.allControls.filter((c) => group.has(c.id))` where `group` is `props.controlIds`, written only by `layout.setterMaps` (auto-sheets.ts:531) and `moveToSetter`. Nothing updates the groups when a control is created, placed, or added to the scoped course afterwards, and neither `courseMap.list` validation, `course.checks` nor `maps.pdf?setter=1` reports scope sites that belong to no setter sheet. The UI row only says 'Setter n · k controls'.
- **Scenario:** Setter maps are generated on Monday (3 groups, 42 controls). On Wednesday the course setter adds control 87 to a course. On Friday 'Export > Setter maps' prints three sheets with the original 42 controls; 87 is on none of them, nobody is told, and the control is not put out in the forest.
- **Fix (M):** Compute, per scope, `setterScopeSites(...)` minus the union of the scope's `props.controlIds`; (a) return it from `courseMap.list` / the Setter section as an 'N controls in no setter map' issue with a one-click 'add to nearest setter' or Regenerate, and (b) make `maps.pdf?setter=1` fail with a 400 naming the controls (or append them to the nearest group's sheet by centroid). Add an integration test in setter-maps.test.ts: generate, add a control, expect the issue. …
- **Status:** confirmed. Code read only (no reproduction run; node_modules absent and the logic is a plain set filter). Read packages/shared/src/course-maps/sheet-rows.ts:271-302, packages/api/src/course-maps/auto-sheets.ts:490-560 and 590-646 …
- **Full entry:** [CRS-002](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-002-setter-maps-are-a-frozen-control-list-controls-added-or-placed-after-generation-appear-on-no-setter-map-with-no-warning)

#### EVT-07 Every Purple Pen (.ppen) export is malformed XML

*Area 07 (Eventor and import/export) · functional, tests that cannot fail · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/api/src/ppen-course-export.ts:229-233`: builder options without `suppressBooleanAttributes: false`. The `"true"` attributes are at `:139`, `:186`, `:200`, `:205` and `:207`. …
- **What is wrong:** with the fast-xml-parser default, an attribute whose value is `"true"` is written bare. Every export, even one with no courses, contains bare `restrict-to-page-size`, `left-to-right`, `scale-sizes-circle-gaps` and `blend-purple`. The tests (`__tests__/ppen-course-export.test.ts:53-60`) only use `toContain` on other substrings. They round-trip through Oxygen's own parser, which silently drops bare attributes.
- **Scenario:** Courses → Export → Purple Pen gives `<print-area automatic="false" restrict-to-page-size left="1"/>`, which a conforming XML reader rejects.
- **Fix (S):** add `suppressBooleanAttributes: false`. Add a test that `XMLValidator.validate(xml) === true` and that `restrict-to-page-size="true"` is present. Open one file in real Purple Pen once after the fix.
- **Status:** Confirmed 12.1 (the pinned version) with the repo's options. Python expat rejects the output ("not well-formed"), and the library's own validator flags it. With the option set, the output is well-formed. That Purple Pen itself rejects the file is inferred.
- **Full entry:** [EVT-07](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-07-every-purple-pen-ppen-export-is-malformed-xml)

#### API-16 Eventor directory and browse procedures are public: whole runner directory and the club's Eventor key reachable without an invite

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · security · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MC-26](areas/02-multi-competition.md#mc-26-unauthenticated-publicprocedures-expose-the-runner-directory-spend-the-eventor-key-and-create-events), [DB-24](areas/03-prisma-migrations.md#db-24-unauthenticated-endpoints-expose-personal-data-and-use-the-stored-eventor-key), [EVT-12](areas/07-eventor.md#evt-12-runner-directory-and-eventor-proxy-endpoints-are-publicprocedure)
- **Related:** API-34, API-37g
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/eventor.ts:582` (`events`), `:604` (`searchEvents`), `:630` (`eventDetail`), `:1220` (`syncRunnerDb`, mutation), `:1363` (`runnerDbStatus`), `:1376` (`searchRunnerDb`), `:1421` (`lookupByCardNo`), `:1447` …
- **What is wrong:** `publicProcedure` only logs errors; none of these handlers reads `ctx`. `docs/system/authentication.md` does not list them as open. `runnerDbDump` returns every directory row (name, card number, club, birth year, sex; about 9 MB, `packages/web/src/hooks/useStationSync.ts:36`); `clubMembers` and `eventDetail` query Eventor with the instance key for any organisation or event id; `syncRunnerDb` downloads the full competitor cache and upserts both directories with no in-flight guard; `fetchEntryHistory` with `force: true` re-fetches up to 20 events per call.
- **Scenario:** proxy mode, an identity that passes the proxy but is not in `users` (or a deactivated user) calls `eventor.runnerDbDump` and receives the personal-data dump; looping `eventor.syncRunnerDb` spends Eventor quota and DB writes on the API box. The verification sweep confirmed all of them reach the handler or input validation with `user: null`.
- **Fix (S):** the kiosk needs `runnerDbDump` without a user (`KioskPage.tsx:109` uses `useStationSync`, which prefetches it at `useStationSync.ts:37,69`; also `StartStation.tsx:11`, `FinishStation.tsx:23`), so use a kiosk-key-or-user gate for `runnerDbDump` (all callers are inside an event, so `x-competition-id` is present and `kioskOrCapProcedure("race.operate")` fits), or stop the kiosk requesting it if it does not use the data. `lookupByCardNo`, `searchRunnerDb`, `clubMembers` (called from `RegistrationDialog.tsx:99-137` in the authed shell): `authedProcedure`. `events` (`CompetitionSelector.tsx:740`, no event) and `fetchEntryHistory` (`RegistrationTrendsPage.tsx:85`): `authedProcedure` …
- **Status:** Confirmed (from code; every line checked; router sweep reached each handler with no identity).
- **Full entry:** [API-16](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-16-eventor-directory-and-browse-procedures-are-public-whole-runner-directory-and-the-clubs-eventor-key-reachable-without-an-invite)

#### API-17 Any signed-in user can obtain any club-library OCAD source by re-adding its hash

*Area 01 (API routers and auth) · security · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [MAP-4](areas/08-vector-map.md#map-4-imp-2-any-signed-in-user-can-obtain-another-uploaders-original-map-file-by-quoting-its-hash)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/routers/clubMap.ts:126-144` (`addFromSource`), `packages/api/src/routers/clubMap.ts:80-85` (uploader overwrite at `:83`), `packages/api/src/routers/clubMap.ts:237-268` (`download`) …
- **What is wrong:** `clubMap.list` (any signed-in user) returns every version's `sourceHash`. `addFromSource` checks only that the `map_sources` row exists. `startImportJob` finds the existing document and returns `done` at once, and `createClubMap` then sets `uploadedBy` to the caller. `download` and `GET /api/map-sources/:hash` then pass the uploader check.
- **Scenario:** two non-admin users, auth on. The uploader uploads `e2e/test.ocd`; the stranger's `download` is FORBIDDEN; the stranger takes `version.sourceHash` from `list`, calls `addFromSource({fileHash})`, then `download({id: <own new series>})` and receives bytes equal to the original file.
- **Fix (M):** gate `addFromSource` on possession: a recent own `map_uploads` row with `finalisedHash = fileHash`, `uploadedBy = ctx.user.id` and `eventId = null`, or instance admin; otherwise FORBIDDEN with "upload it again" (upload rows are purged after 24 h, `jobs.ts:492`). Skip the check when `!ctx.authEnabled` (`uploadedBy` is null on both sides). Hiding `sourceHash` from `list` alone does not fix it. Set the version's uploader from the job's `startedBy` in `applyJobResult` instead of the post-hoc `updateMany`; that also fixes the opposite case where a legitimate second uploader whose import outlasts the 30 s hold cannot download their own series. …
- **Status:** Confirmed (reproduced: throwaway integration test, 1/1 passed). The `applyUploadedMap` sibling is plausible only.
- **Full entry:** [API-17](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-17-any-signed-in-user-can-obtain-any-club-library-ocad-source-by-re-adding-its-hash)

#### CRS-003 Stored XSS through SVG graphics: the validator checks a stripped copy, the original bytes are stored and served as image/svg+xml without CSP

*Area 09 (Courses, printing and elevation) · security · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Also reported as:** [API-31](areas/01-api-auth.md#api-31-uploaded-svg-graphics-are-served-same-origin-as-imagesvgxml-behind-a-bypassable-regex-blocklist-stored-xss)
- **Location:** `packages/api/src/course-maps/graphics.ts:62 (checked at 73-77; bytes stored at 166-174; served at packages/api/src/course-maps/routes.ts:521-524)`; also packages/api/src/course-maps/routes.ts:521-524, 564-567, 612-615
- **What is wrong:** parseSvgGraphic runs SVG_FORBIDDEN against `sanitizeSvgMarkup(text)`, which first removes `<metadata>…</metadata>`, the DOCTYPE and processing instructions, but prepareGraphicUpload stores the uploaded bytes (or the trim wrapper around them) unchanged. So content inside what the sanitiser strips is never checked. …
- **Scenario:** A user with `courses.edit` on any one event uploads the metadata/entity SVG with scope 'club' (visible to every event). They send an instance admin the link /api/maps/<event>/graphics/<id>. The admin's browser renders the SVG document, the script runs with the admin's identity and can call any tRPC mutation (delete events, change permissions, export runner data).
- **Fix (S):** Serve every graphic response (graphics/:id, and defensively print.png / thumb.png) with `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; sandbox`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`; optionally `Content-Disposition: inline; filename=…` stays. In the validator, run SVG_FORBIDDEN on the raw decoded text (not the stripped copy), also reject `<!ENTITY` values containing `<`/`&#60;`/`&lt;` followed by markup, and reject `href`/`src` values that are not `#fragment` or `data:image/`. Pass resvg `resourcesDir` unset plus an image-href resolver that refuses non-data URLs if the binding allows. Add the two payloads above to course-map-graphics.test.ts. Verifier: Fix both ends. …
- **Status:** confirmed. Code read plus a standalone node reproduction of the validator. Files read: packages/api/src/course-maps/graphics.ts:31-41, 53-77, 160-175; packages/api/src/course-maps/svg-window.ts:37-52, 92-116; packages/api/src/routers/graphics.ts:90-124 …
- **Full entry:** [CRS-003](areas/09-courses-printing.md#crs-003-stored-xss-through-svg-graphics-the-validator-checks-a-stripped-copy-the-original-bytes-are-stored-and-served-as-imagesvgxml-without-csp)

#### MAP-1 (PR 254) Official course climb from the plain contour field rests on a hard-coded contour interval that nothing can override

*Area 08 (Vector map pipeline) · functional · **high** · size M · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/shared/src/terrain/index.ts:34-36` (`contourIntervalM`: `ISSprOM*` gives 2.5 m, anything else 5 m). `packages/api/src/map-store/heightfield-worker.ts:21` and `packages/api/src/map-store/dem-worker.ts:54` (both call without …
- **What is wrong:** every height in a `contours` field is `level × intervalM`, and `intervalM` is never read from the map or settable per map. PR 254 makes the climb from this field the course's stored, printed and Eventor-exported climb whenever no anchor-grade ground model exists. A coarse-DEM anchored field is also affected, for chains without a DEM height. The error is the ratio of the assumed interval to the true one. …
- **Scenario:** an ISOM forest map drawn with 2.5 m contours and no DEM (not georeferenced, outside coverage, or no credential). A course with 120 m of true climb is stored, printed and exported as about 240 m. A sprint map with 2 m contours reads 125 %; an ISOM map with 10 m contours reads 50 %.
- **Fix (M):** Make the interval a per-map value. Read it from OMAP, where the file has it. OCAD does not store it reliably (research 08 line 218), so ask at import or in the map settings. Store it on the document header or the map row, and pass it as `opts.intervalM` from both workers and from `dem-live-check.ts:68`. Bump `TERRAIN_VERSION` or key the artefact on the interval. Until the interval is confirmed, PR 254 should treat a contours-basis climb as not storable (as it already does for a partial profile, decision 11), or at least print the assumed interval next to the number. Surface `intervalSuspect` in Height data settings. Size: M (L with the import question UI).
- **Status:** confirmed, proven from the code. The reviewer read the terrain and profile code and grepped both branches. The verifier repeated the grep and traced every derive caller.
- **Full entry:** [MAP-1](areas/08-vector-map.md#map-1-ter-2-pr-254-official-course-climb-from-the-plain-contour-field-rests-on-a-hard-coded-contour-interval-that-nothing-can-override)

#### WEB-12 Registration trends: adding a comparison event starts a request storm that never stops if the fetch fails

*Area 10 (Web client) · stability · **high** · size S · confirmed*

- **Location:** `packages/web/src/pages/RegistrationTrendsPage.tsx:83-85` (the two `useMutation` hooks). `packages/web/src/pages/RegistrationTrendsPage.tsx:87-152` (`loadComparison`, deps at `:152` …
- **What is wrong:** `loadComparison` is a `useCallback` whose deps are the mutation result objects, and React Query returns a new object on every render. So the callback changes identity every render and the effect re-runs after every render. Whenever a selected comparison has no entry in `comparisonData`, the effect calls `mutateAsync`, which dispatches "pending", notifies the observer and re-renders, which runs the effect again. The loop ends only when a response is written to `comparisonData`. …
- **Scenario:** the operator adds a comparison event, or opens a `?cmp=` URL, on a slow arena link. The page fires `fetchComparison` once per render tick until the first answer lands, then many of those answers trigger their own Eventor fetch. If the browser is online but the API is down or returns 5xx or FORBIDDEN (the usual case of LAN up, server down), the tab keeps issuing failing requests until it is closed. …
- **Fix (S):** Destructure `mutateAsync` from each hook; it is stable for the observer's lifetime. Stable deps alone are not enough: keep a `requestedRef: Set<number>` so each id is requested once, record failures as `comparisonData[id] = { entries: [], error }`, and add a catch. Clear the ref entry on a forced Refresh (`:403`) and when a comparison is removed. Better: make `fetchComparison` a query keyed by ids. Add a server-side in-flight guard on `eventor.fetchEntryHistory`. Test: an E2E that adds a comparison with the route stubbed slow and asserts exactly one `fetchComparison` request, and one with the route failing.
- **Status:** confirmed, from code reading of the page, React Query 5.107.1 and @trpc/react-query 11.28.0 `useMutation`, and the two API procedures, and by a scratch vitest (`packages/web/src/__scratch_v_C_C3__/storm.test.tsx`, deleted) mounting the same hook structure …
- **Full entry:** [WEB-12](areas/10-web-client.md#web-12-registration-trends-adding-a-comparison-event-starts-a-request-storm-that-never-stops-if-the-fetch-fails)

## Clean

No area was clean as a whole. These parts were read closely and had no finding (details in each area report's "Clean" section):

- **API (01):** `auth.ts`, `permissions.ts`, `restGuard.ts`, `secret-box.ts`, `eventorKeyStore.ts`, `staticServe.ts`, `googleIdToken.ts`; the map, course-map and export REST routes; raw SQL (no injection anywhere); the auth integration tests (none that cannot fail).
- **Multi-competition (02):** event scoping in every router except the listed exceptions; module-level caches; service-worker cache partitioning; BroadcastChannels; Dexie tables other than `projRunners`.
- **Schema (03):** migrations agree with `schema.prisma` on every column; ordering; destructive migrations have guards; every event table cascades and every unique constraint is event-scoped; map-store transactions and GC.
- **Results and draw (04):** `statusConvert.ts`, `serverClock.ts`, `matchPunchesToCourse` and `computeClassPlacements` within one day, the draw optimizer (8 ms for 2,040 runners) and `randomDraw`.
- **SI readout (05):** `reader-hub.ts`, the Bluetooth decoder and its tests, CRC computation, the SI5 and SI10/SIAC layouts, competition scoping inside `cardReadout.ts`.
- **Offline sync (06):** `hlc.ts`, `journal.ts`, the leader lease SQL, the snapshot map and graphics refresh, the control outbox, the service-worker update flow (no forced reload mid-event), secrets handling.
- **Eventor (07):** key store, directory upsert SQL, `eventor-pace.ts`, XML escaping and entity safety, environment selection, export route and LiveResults router authorisation.
- **Vector map (08):** the map-document codec, patch merge and apply, server patch writes and promote, GC, colour-settings locking, height-field routes, the field codec and solve, the OMAP/XMAP reader. No finding loses or corrupts a stored map document or patch.
- **Courses and printing (09):** course/control/class router scoping and gating, structure and variant materialisation, the print composer and writer maths, climb maths in PR #254.
- **Web client (10):** the shell helpers, most hooks, i18n key parity, no secrets in the bundle, no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` on user data, lazy routes and chunking.
- **Build and tests (11):** version stamping, Postgres 18 volume paths, E2E shard split, the E2E gate, `reuseExistingServer: false` everywhere, global setup, `audit-prod.mjs`, E2E waits.

## Left out on purpose

- **Refuted by a verifier and dropped:** the runner card check-then-insert race (a partial unique index prevents it; residual kept as MC-56); `events.ts:356` as a `> 0` test (it is `=== 0`); ROC duplicate punches (the dedupe index exists; the real failure is the stall in SYNC-05); several sub-claims in areas 06 and 11 (each area report lists them); `clubMap.rename` open to everyone (documented); `leg_routes` lacking an `updated_at` trigger; the "duplicate" registration-dialog and kiosk-readout E2E pair.
- **Already listed as pending or documented as intended:** area 09's three refuted findings (re-importing Oxygen's own forked export, the editor orchestration having no tests, queued moves keyed by the public code), leg routes drawn at the venue not returning at checkin (docs/course-elevation.md Pending, decision 19; area 06's SYNC-33 says the same and is kept only as a cross-reference), club key 0 for clubless runners in the draw, and the club-wide library rows documented in docs/system/club-library.md.
- **Not worth a fix:** the init migration citing a missing `docs/schema.md` (editing an applied migration changes its checksum).
- Style, formatting, linter output and the known `oxygen_club_logo` Prisma build errors were out of scope.

## Not reviewed, or could not be verified

These are the gaps across all areas. Each area report has its own list with file names.

- **Little was run.** Areas 04, 05, 06, 07, 09 and 11 had no `node_modules` (the `ocad2geojson` git tarball blocked `pnpm install` in those containers), so no vitest, integration, E2E, tsc, Prisma or Docker run was possible there; their findings are proven from code, some with standalone scripts. Area 08 reproduced its terrain and import findings with scratch scripts against the real modules, but ran no integration, browser or GPU test. Areas 01, 03 and 06 reproduced a few findings on a throwaway PostgreSQL 16 on :5433, not on the full migration chain. Area 10 had `node_modules` and ran scratch vitests and one `vite build`. Nowhere was a browser, an E2E suite, a two-node venue + cloud setup or a full migrated database used.
- **No hardware.** SI9 and pCard punch offsets (SI-04), what a BSM station sends when a card is pulled mid-read (SI-05), Sunday in the TD byte (SI-27), series byte 1 for low SI5 numbers (SI-26), the 0xD3 frame layout (SI-42), station behaviour on bogus SI5 block requests (SI-28), receipt printers on paper-out and their code pages (CRS-044, CRS-046), and Web Serial error behaviour (SI-18).
- **External systems not observed.** Whether Eventor ever answers 200 with no `EntryList` (EVT-01), sends entries without a PersonId (MC-34), accepts an Id-less class or the `Course` element (EVT-14, EVT-22), and whether Test-Eventor shares person ids with production (EVT-15); the LiveResults remote schema and its `changed` semantics (SYNC-15, RES-23); real ROC id ranges and duplicate behaviour (SYNC-26); IAP and Cloud Run responses (SYNC-23, API-29, BLD-01 end to end); gcloud's replace semantics (BLD-02, from documentation).
- **Live state not checked.** Whether production has the SQL-only triggers and partial indexes, whether point-in-time recovery is on (DB-35), whether `authEnabled` is on for venue boxes (decides how bad WEB-04 is), repository settings (Dependabot alerts, required checks, branch protection).
- **Performance was reasoned, not measured.** Render cost of the 2,000-row tables, payload sizes, `resultList` and `controlCompletionStatus` server cost, LiveResults cycle time over the WAN, interactive-transaction timings, event-delete scan cost, peak memory of large map imports, and real compression timings. Only the bundle sizes (WEB-76) and the draw optimizer were measured.
- **Dependency vulnerabilities.** No network audit was possible and the lockfile versions post-date the reviewers' knowledge, so no CVE claims are made; the unconditional pnpm overrides were not checked for forcing an incompatible major.
- **Code not read, or only skimmed.** The largest gaps:
  - Map import symbol matching and colour layering (`packages/shared/src/import/matcher.ts`, `fingerprint.ts`, `colour-*.ts`, `ocad-symbols.ts`, most of `omap-symbols.ts`, `report.ts`): searched for loops and type holes only. Area 08 recommends a follow-up review.
  - The Map-mode editor UI (`pages/editor/modes/map/**`, `tracks/`, `CompareView.tsx`, `ColourLayersPanel.tsx`) and most course-editor tools and layout files beyond greps.
  - The `ocad2geojson` fork beyond `ocad-reader/index.js`, `object-index.js` and `buffer-reader.js`; OMAP was not fuzzed.
  - Course geometry drawing (`packages/shared/src/course/corridor.ts`, `cuts.ts`, `bends.ts`, `arcs.ts`, `symbols.ts` and others) and the route calibration code of PR #254.
  - `scripts/migrate-mysql-to-pg.ts` (where ZeroTime of migrated events comes from), the draw for forked variants with different first controls, `testLab.ts` after line 560, parts of `routers/event.ts` and `routers/eventor.ts`, `registrationTrends.ts`.
  - The test suites of the map, course, print, render, import, DEM and symbol-library packages (about 200 of 304 test files) and the matching web unit tests and E2E specs were swept with greps only, so test-quality findings there are missing.
- **Open questions left by verifiers.** Whether a radio finish stored under code 2 feeds results (API-21); whether `mapImport.review`, `mapImport.exportCrt` and `lease.checkout` guard inside the handler (API-36); how a non-holder's `course.upserted` merges on the venue during a lease (MC-36); whether `navigator.onLine` is false on a venue laptop with no uplink but a reachable local API (DB-11); how offline clients apply `course.upserted { removed: true }` (DB-17); `maxParamLength: 500` against an `httpBatchLink` with no `maxURLLength` (plausible only).
- **Leads not verified** (from area 06): a whole-batch zod rejection blocking the station queue for good; an unguarded `toRelative` in the `result.applied` apply giving a DNF a non-zero time; `useProjectionSync` hydrating competition B's projection from A's cache; event-row settings mutations on the box being overwritten by the settings refresh (see MC-08); the projection treating the registration `clubId` as `eventorClubId`.
- **PR #254 is still moving.** Its findings (MAP-1, API-15, DB-53, CRS-048, CRS-070 to CRS-072, CRS-089, CRS-090, EVT-22, SYNC-17's PR part) are against `871c74de` and may already be stale on the branch.
